Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biogeocenology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 792. The projective resolution has length at most 331 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to biogeocenology structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 792/331 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 244 is immediate from the entropy bound property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biogeocenology structure using the entropy bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 498. Each subproblem has a solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
